  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\lnp\\Documents\\Obsidian Vault\\80 知识库\\具身智能\\_主题页.md",
  "content": "# 具身智能 · Physical Intelligence 公司技术路线\n\n> 主题页 | AI 维护 | Updated: 2026-05-23\n> 涵盖 Physical Intelligence 公司的 VLA 模型系列（π0/Hi Robot/π0.5/π*0.6/π0.7），以及支持这些主线模型的分支技术（FAST/RTC/MEM/RLT）\n\n---\n\n## 这个主题在讨论什么\n\n**具身智能（Embodied Intelligence / Physical Intelligence）**：让 AI 系统通过物理身体与真实世界交互，完成像人类一样的日常任务。这跟下棋、写文章、画图这些\"虚拟智能\"是两个领域——物理世界有不可压缩的复杂度（**Moravec 悖论**：对 AI 来说，下棋容易、叠衣服难）。\n\n**VLA（Vision-Language-Action Model）** 是当前主流范式：从 LLM/VLM 出发，扩展出连续电机动作输出，让一个模型同时理解视觉、语言和动作。\n\n## Physical Intelligence 公司：五代主线 VLA 演进\n\n| 时间 | 模型 | 突破点 |\n|------|------|--------|\n| 2024-10 | **π0** | VLM (3B) + flow matching，首个通用机器人基础模型，控制 8 种机器人 |\n| 2025-02 | **Hi Robot** | 分层 VLA：System 2 推理 + System 1 执行，比 GPT-4o 高 40% 指令跟随 |\n| 2025-04 | **π0.5** | 单模型双输出（token + flow matching）+ 异构数据 co-training → open-world 泛化 |\n| 2025-11 | **π*0.6** | 基于 π0.6（5B backbone）+ Recap RL → 实用级 throughput（工厂连续作业） |\n| 2026-04 | **π0.7** | **emergent compositional generalization** + steerable prompts，单模型替代多 specialist |\n\n| 模型 | 关键问题 | 关键创新 | 主要任务 |\n|------|---------|---------|---------|\n| **π0** | 一个模型能控制多种机器人吗？ | VLM + flow matching | 叠衣服、收拾桌、装盒子 |\n| **Hi Robot** | 能处理复杂多步指令吗？ | 分层 VLA + 40% 高于 GPT-4o | 清桌子、做三明治、超市采购 |\n| **π0.5** | 能在没见过的家里干活吗？ | 异构数据 co-training | 全新家里清厨房卧室 |\n| **π*0.6** | 能从自己实践中改进吗？ | Recap = RL + corrections + experience | 工厂级连续作业 |\n| **π0.7** | 能组合已学技能完成新任务吗？ | Steerable prompts（language + metadata + visual subgoal）+ 多源数据蒸馏 | 用没见过的空气炸锅、跨机器人形态叠衣服 |\n\n## 分支技术（支持主线 VLA 的组件）\n\n主线模型的能力依赖于若干基础技术。这些技术不是新模型，而是让主线模型跑得更好/更快/更省/更稳的\"零件\"。\n\n| 时间 | 技术 | 用途 | 一句话 |\n|------|------|------|--------|\n| 2025-01 | **FAST** | 动作 tokenization | DCT 频域压缩替代 binning，π0-FAST 训练**快 5 倍** |\n| 2025-06 | **RTC** | 实时执行 | 异步生成 + inpaint，让大型 VLA 在 300ms+ 延迟下也能精确动作 |\n| 2026-03 | **MEM** | 长时记忆 | 短期视频 + 长期语言双模态，支持 15 分钟长任务 |\n| 2026-03 | **RLT** | 精细 RL | 在 VLA 上加 RL token，几小时数据就能让精细动作加速 3x |\n\n**两条 RL 路线**：\n- **Recap（π*0.6）= 宽**：大规模 RL 提升整体性能\n- **RLT = 窄**：轻量 RL 精细补刀\n\n## 技术演进的关键决策\n\n### 1. 从离散 token 输出到连续动作输出（π0）\n传统 VLM 输出文字 token，但机器人需要 50Hz 的电机指令。π0 引入 flow matching（diffusion 变体）作为 action expert，让 VLM 能输出连续动作。\n\n### 2. 从单模型推理到分层推理（Hi Robot）\n复杂指令（\"清理桌子，但别动那个红色玩具\"）超出 π0 的能力。Hi Robot 加一个高层 VLM 做规划和反馈处理，让机器人有\"inner voice\"。\n\n### 3. 从分层模型回归单模型（π0.5）\nπ0.5 把分层思想内化：同一个模型既能输出 high-level 文字命令，也能输出 low-level 动作。架构更简单、效果更好。\n\n### 4. 从同环境训练到 open-world 泛化（π0.5）\n之前的 VLA 训练和测试在相似环境。π0.5 通过 co-training 异构数据，第一次实现\"在训练完全没见过的家\"里完成长任务。\n\n### 5. 从纯监督学习到 RL + 经验学习（π*0.6）\n监督学习有**复合错误**问题——小错误累积成大失败。π*0.6 用 **Recap** 三阶段训练：示范 + 纠错 + 自主经验（advantage-conditioned RL）。结果：吞吐量翻倍，失败率减半，成功率 90%+，能工厂级连续作业。\n\n### 6. 从 task-specific specialist 到 steerable 通用模型（π0.7）\nπ*0.6 每个任务训一个 specialist；π0.7 把所有 specialist 的能力**蒸馏**到单一通用模型。关键技术是**多样化 prompt 条件**——language + metadata（speed/quality）+ control modality + visual subgoals。\n\n## 主要洞察\n\n1. **泛化 > 灵巧**：行业焦虑过度集中在炫技（杂技、跳舞），但真正的难题是\"在新厨房洗碗\"\n2. **混合数据 >

Here are the search results for your query about Physical Intelligence's RL for Generalist Policies research:

## Physical Intelligence Blog Posts

**π*0.6: A VLA that Learns from Experience** (Primary result)
This appears to be the main blog post matching your query. It describes a method for training generalist policies with RL to improve success rate and throughput on real-world tasks.

Key technical details from the paper:
- They present a general-purpose method called RECAP (RL with Experience and Corrections via Advantage-conditioned Policies), which provides for RL training of VLAs via advantage conditioning. The method incorporates heterogeneous data into the self-improvement process, including demonstrations, data from on-policy collection, and expert teleoperated interventions provided during autonomous execution.
- Recap enables two ways to get good training signals from "bad" experiential data: coaching to provide corrections, where an expert shows the robot how it can fix a mistake or do better, and reinforcement learning, where the robot judges for itself which of its behaviors were better or worse based on the overall outcome of an episode, and iteratively learns to perform the good behaviors while avoiding the bad ones.
- For coaching to be useful, an expert teleoperator needs to provide corrections showing how to recover from the mistakes that the robot actually makes in the real world. In practice, this means running their best current policy and "taking over" with manual teleoperation when the robot makes a mistake.

**Related Physical Intelligence work** mentioned on their blog:
- They extract an RL Token from VLA models to enable fast online RL and improve throughput on precise tasks with few hours of data.
- Their first generalist policy, π0, is a prototype model that combines large-scale multi-task and multi-robot data collection with a new network architecture to enable a capable and dexterous generalist robot policy.

## Related Research

**RLDG: Robotic Generalist Policy Distillation via Reinforcement Learning** (UC Berkeley)
This work proposes Reinforcement Learning Distilled Generalists (RLDG), a method that leverages reinforcement learning to generate high-quality training data for fine-tuning generalist policies. Through real-world experiments on precise manipulation tasks like connector insertion and assembly, they demonstrate that generalist policies trained with RL-generated data consistently outperform those trained with human demonstrations, achieving up to 40% higher success rates while generalizing better to new tasks.

**Background context from Sergey Levine's CoRL 2024 talk:**
The talk covered π0, a generalist robot policy capable of handling multiple morphologies and specializing in dexterous manipulation; how to leverage the language generation capabilities of vision-language models to improve reasoning; and how to achieve better performance by using reinforcement learning during the post-training phase.

**Key sources:**
- Physical Intelligence blog post: https://www.pi.website/blog/pistar06
- Paper PDF: https://www.pi.website/download/pistar06.pdf
- Main blog index: https://www.pi.website/blog

Here are the search results for Physical Intelligence's research on robot learning from human videos:

## Physical Intelligence's "Human to Robot Transfer" Paper

The paper you're looking for is titled "Emergence of Human to Robot Transfer in Vision-Language-Action Models," conducted in collaboration with the Georgia Institute of Technology.

### Key Findings

The San Francisco startup says its research shows that robots can learn new tasks from first-person human demonstration videos, but with an important caveat: the approach only works once the robot's underlying artificial intelligence has been trained on enough diverse real-world experiences. Once that critical mass is met, the researchers say, videos of humans become a powerful training resource rather than background noise.

### The "Emergent" Property

In a research update, Pi disclosed that its Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, such as π₀.₅, exhibit an "emergent property" as they scale: they spontaneously learn to align human movements with robot actions. This finding suggests that the "domain gap"—the notorious difficulty of teaching robots by showing them videos of human hands—might not require complex translation layers or specialized hardware like Sunday Robotics' gloves.

### Scale Matters

The research revealed a stark contrast between model sizes:
- Small Models struggled to learn from the human video, viewing the human hands as foreign objects unrelated to the robot's grippers.
- The π₀.₅ model, when fine-tuned on the same human video, achieved roughly 2x the performance on generalization tasks.

"We were surprised," the company wrote. "We did not include any special mechanism to facilitate transfer."

### Performance Results

In household cleanup tasks, success rates dramatically improved:
- Tidying a spice rack improved from 32 to 71 percent, while cleaning a dresser surface rose from 25 to 50 percent.
- For a more complex table-bussing job, performance improved a modest 10 points, from 53 to 63 percent.

### Visualization of the Bridge

The overlap between human video data and robot data in latent space projections indicates that the scaled-up model treats human and robot actions as mathematically similar. The company released 2D projection plots to visualize this phenomenon.

### Researchers' Outlook

"Much like large language models, larger VLAs may not only improve performance but also unlock entirely new capabilities," the researchers wrote. "Using human video may be just one such capability, and it's exciting to imagine what others might emerge as we continue to scale up robotic foundation models."

The researchers said the key pattern across tests was that human video only helped once the model had enough prior knowledge. This aligns with Physical Intelligence's broader philosophy: the company consistently argues that learning grounded in real-life physical interaction matters more than relying on simulation training and synthetic data.

## Most Relevant Result

**"A VLA that Learns from Experience" — Physical Intelligence (π*0.6 / Recap)**

The most directly relevant result from Physical Intelligence itself describes their recent work on tackling difficult manipulation tasks. They studied three applications: making espresso drinks, folding diverse laundry, and assembling boxes for packaging. Their approach, called Recap, involves a multi-stage training pipeline: the first stage is to pre-train the π*0.6 model with offline RL, in contrast to the standard supervised learning method used by the base π0.6 and π0.5 VLAs. From there, they fine-tune π*0.6 to each task with demonstrations, and then train it further with RL using additional data collected on the robot, with both expert-provided corrections to fix large mistakes and feedback from rewards to improve from autonomous experience.

Source: https://www.pi.website/blog/pistar06

## Other Related VLA Fine-Tuning Papers Found

While these aren't from Physical Intelligence specifically, they address similar challenges in fine-tuning VLAs for difficult manipulation:

1. **CRAFT: Adapting VLA Models to Contact-rich Manipulation via Force-aware Curriculum Fine-tuning** — Addresses the challenge that contact-rich manipulation remains a fundamental challenge for VLA models, as tasks such as precise insertions, deformable object rolling, or continuous surface wiping require precise alignment and dynamic regulation of contact forces, yet existing models often fail to meet these demands. The paper notes that recent approaches, including RDT and π0, further underscore the potential of unifying large-scale multimodal pretraining with robot control.

2. **Dual-Actor Fine-Tuning of VLA Models (Talk-and-Tweak)** — VLA models demonstrate strong generalization in robotic manipulation but face challenges in complex, real-world tasks. While supervised finetuning with demonstrations is constrained by data quality, RL offers a promising alternative.

3. **VLA-RL** — Enables OpenVLA-7B to surpass the strongest finetuned baseline by 4.5% on 40 challenging robotic manipulation tasks in LIBERO, and even matches the performance of advanced commercial models such as π0-FAST.

4. **TacVLA** — Addresses suboptimal VLA performance in tasks involving visual occlusion, fine-grained manipulation, and physical contact by proposing a fine-tuned VLA model incorporating tactile modalities into the transformer-based policy.

5. **AirVLA** — The first demonstration of a pretrained VLA fine-tuned for an aerial manipulation platform.

## Paper: "Knowledge Insulating Vision-Language-Action Models: Train Fast, Run Fast, Generalize Better"

**Authors & Publication**: The paper is by Danny Driess, Jost Tobias Springenberg, Brian Ichter, Lili Yu, Adrian Li-Bell, Karl Pertsch, Allen Z. Ren, Homer Walke, Quan Vuong, Lucy Xiaoyang Shi, and Sergey Levine from Physical Intelligence. It was published on 5/30/2025 (arXiv:2505.23705) and accepted as a NeurIPS 2025 poster.

### Core Problem
Vision-language-action (VLA) models combine end-to-end learning with transfer of semantic knowledge from web-scale VLM training. However, the constraints of real-time control are often at odds with the design of VLMs: the most powerful VLMs have tens or hundreds of billions of parameters, presenting an obstacle to real-time inference, and operate on discrete tokens rather than the continuous-valued outputs that are required for controlling robots.

### Key Insight
Recent VLA models have used specialized modules for efficient continuous control, such as action experts or continuous output heads, which typically require adding new untrained parameters to the pretrained VLM backbone. While these modules improve real-time and control capabilities, it remains an open question whether they preserve or degrade the semantic knowledge contained in the pretrained VLM. The paper shows that naively including such experts significantly harms both training speed and knowledge transfer.

These second-generation VLAs could perform much more complex tasks, but adding new motor control weights to the model during VLA finetuning resulted in complex learning dynamics that could damage the VLM's internal representations. Essentially, grafting this "motor cortex" onto the VLM in such a crude way could cause a kind of "forgetfulness," slowing down training significantly and harming the model's eventual ability to interpret language.

### Proposed Solution: Knowledge Insulation
The authors provide an extensive analysis of various design choices, their impact on performance and knowledge transfer, and propose a technique for insulating the VLM backbone during VLA training that mitigates this issue.

The method involves three components: co-training, joint-training, and knowledge insulation, including joint discrete/continuous action prediction—training the model to simultaneously predict both discretized actions (using FAST tokenization) via a language modeling-style objective and continuous actions via flow matching using an action expert.

### Why Prior Approaches Fall Short
- Autoregressive VLAs (like π0-FAST) are slow due to sequential decoding and tokenization overhead, limiting control frequency.
- Robotics-specific modules (like action experts in π0) are initialized from scratch and trained with gradients from robotics data, which can degrade VLM knowledge, harming language interpretation and performance. Freezing the VLM backbone prevents this degradation but results in insufficient representations for robotics tasks as VLMs are not pre-trained on such data.

### Results & Trade-offs
- Achieves faster training, reduced computational costs, and better real-world performance, with significant improvements in robo

The authors describe that the model can be used directly to perform tasks based on a prompt, or fine-tuned on high-quality data to enable complex multi-stage tasks, such as folding multiple articles of laundry or assembling a box.

The paper's broader contribution is described as follows:

- The model is trained on a large and diverse dataset from multiple dexterous robot platforms—including single-arm robots, dual-arm robots, and mobile manipulators—and is evaluated on its ability to perform tasks via direct prompting, follow language instructions from people and from a high-level VLM policy, and acquire new skills via fine-tuning.

The model is pre-trained on diverse data from 7 distinct robot configurations and 68 tasks, and can then either be prompted directly or fine-tuned to complex downstream tasks, as in the case of this laundry folding policy, which fetches laundry from a dryer, packs it into a hamper, brings the hamper to a folding table, and then folds each article of clothing.

  "content": "---\nsource: \"70 素材/具身智能/Human Video Transfer-stub-2025.md\"\ndate: 2026-05-23\ntopic: 具身智能\n---\n\n# Human Video Transfer：VLA scale up 后，\"看人类做\"会涌现地变成训练资源\n\n> Physical Intelligence + Georgia Tech 发布。研究发现 VLA 模型 scale 到 π0.5 这种规模后，会**涌现性地**学会从人类第一视角视频里学技能——**没有任何特殊机制**。小模型把人类手当\"外星物体\"，π0.5 把人类视频当训练资源，泛化任务表现提升 ~2x。这是 VLA 领域的 \"GPT-3 moment\" 之一：规模带来的能力跃迁。\n\n> ⚠️ 基于 WebSearch 合成，非原文存档。\n\n## 核心论点\n\n- **VLA 的涌现性 = LLM 的涌现性**：跟 LLM 一样，VLA 模型**只有 scale 够大才会出现某些能力**。Human-to-robot transfer 就是其中之一。\n\n- **关键发现**：小模型看人类视频学不到东西（人手对模型来说是\"无关像素\"）；π0.5 同样 fine-tune 同样视频，泛化任务表现**翻倍**。\n\n- **没有特殊机制**：PI 团队没加翻译层、没用 Sunday Robotics 那种数据手套、没做 morphology mapping。**纯靠模型自己学到 align 人类动作和机器人动作**。\n\n- **PI 的话**：\"We did not include any special mechanism to facilitate transfer.\"\n\n- **具体数据**（清理家居）：\n  - 清香料架：32% → 71%\n  - 清桌面：25% → 50%\n  - 桌子 bussing：53% → 63%\n\n- **解释**：在 latent space 投影里，人类视频数据和机器人数据**几何上重叠**，模型把它们当数学上相似的动作。\n\n- **战略意义**：解决了 robot data 稀缺问题的一个潜在方向——**网上有海量人类做家务的视频**，如果能用，VLA 的数据瓶颈大大缓解。\n\n## 关键观点（WebSearch 综合，非原文逐字）\n\n> \"Much like large language models, larger VLAs may not only improve performance but also unlock entirely new capabilities. Using human video may be just one such capability, and it's exciting to imagine what others might emerge as we continue to scale up robotic foundation models.\"\n\n> \"We did not include any special mechanism to facilitate transfer.\"\n\n> \"The overlap between human video data and robot data in latent space projections indicates that the scaled-up model treats human and robot actions as mathematically similar.\"\n\n## 跟主线的位置\n\nHuman Video Transfer 是 **VLA scaling laws 的 evidence**：\n- 之前怀疑 VLA 能不能像 LLM 一样有涌现能力\n- 这个研究给了证据——**至少在\"从人类视频学\"这一项上，π0.5 是涌现的**\n\n跟 π0.7 的关系：π0.7 论文里也提到用 human videos 作为数据源——这个研究为那个用法提供了 scientific backing。\n\n也回应了主题页里的一个问题：**数据稀缺**怎么办？答案之一是\"等模型够大，人类视频就成了数据\"。\n\n## 关键技术细节\n\n- **测试模型**：π0.5\n- **训练数据**：第一视角人类视频\n- **任务**：清香料架、清桌面、bus 桌\n- **核心方法**：fine-tune（不需要特殊架构）\n\n## 我的笔记\n\n（待补充）\n\n## See Also\n\n- [[80 知识库/具身智能/_主题页]] — 主题鸟瞰\n- [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/π0.5-Open-World泛化-2026-05]] — 这个研究的载体模型\n- [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/π0.7-Steerable通用模型-2026-05]] — π0.7 论文里 human videos 是关键数据源之一\n- [[小林的/50 Learning/计划5]] — 学习清单\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\lnp\\Documents\\Obsidian Vault\\80 知识库\\具身智能\\素材\\Knowledge Insulation-VLM保护-2026-05.md",
  "content": "---\nsource: \"70 素材/具身智能/Knowledge Insulation-stub-2025-05.md\"\ndate: 2026-05-23\ntopic: 具身智能\n---\n\n# Knowledge Insulation：保护 VLM 骨架不被 action expert 污染——VLA 训练快 3x\n\n> Physical Intelligence 2025-05 发布（arXiv:2505.23705，NeurIPS 2025 poster）。揭示\"二代 VLA\"（带 action expert 的 π0 类模型）的隐藏问题：新加的 motor 参数会**破坏 VLM 原有的语义知识**——一种 \"forgetfulness\"。提出 **Knowledge Insulation** = co-training + joint-training + 联合离散/连续动作预测。效果：训练快 3x，性能更好，语言理解保护得更好。\n\n> ⚠️ 基于 WebSearch 合成，非原文存档。\n\n## 核心论点\n\n- **二代 VLA 的隐藏代价**：π0 这类模型在 VLM 上加了 action expert（continuous 输出头）。这些**新参数从零初始化**，跟 VLM 一起 fine-tune 时，会\"污染\"原 VLM 的内部表征——损害语言理解和泛化能力。\n\n- **生动比喻**：把\"motor cortex\"硬接在 VLM 上，可能造成\"forgetfulness\"——训练慢、语言能力降级。\n\n- **不能直接 freeze VLM**：那样会失去机器人需要的 robotics 适配；表征不够。\n\n- **解决方案：Knowledge Insulation**，三个组件：\n  1. **Co-training**：跟非机器人数据共训\n  2. **Joint-training**：联合训练多目标\n  3. **Joint discrete/continuous action prediction**：同时预测\n     - 离散动作（用 FAST tokenization）—— language modeling 风格目标\n     - 连续动作（用 flow matching action expert）—— diffusion 风格目标\n\n- **关键洞察**：让模型在 backbone 上\"两条腿走路\"——一边走 FAST 语言路线保住 VLM 知识，一边走 flow matching 路线学连续控制。互相牵制保护核心 weights。\n\n## 性能\n\n- **训练快 3x**（wall-clock）\n- 性能不降反升\n- 语言理解能力**得到保护**\n- 代价：训练计算 +20%（但更快收敛抵消）\n- 局限：语言跟随仍不完美\n\n## 关键观点（WebSearch 综合，非原文逐字）\n\n> \"Vision-language-action (VLA) models combine end-to-end learning with transfer of semantic knowledge from web-scale VLM training. However, the constraints of real-time control are often at odds with the design of VLMs.\"\n\n> \"Adding new motor control weights to the model during VLA finetuning resulted in complex learning dynamics that could damage the VLM's internal representations. Essentially, grafting this 'motor cortex' onto the VLM in such a crude way could cause a kind of 'forgetfulness'.\"\n\n> \"While these modules improve real-time and control capabilities, it remains an open question whether they preserve or degrade the semantic knowledge contained in the pretrained VLM. The paper shows that naively including such experts significantly harms both training speed and knowledge transfer.\"\n\n## 跟主线的位置\n\nKnowledge Insulation 是 **VLA 工程层面的关键洞察**：\n- 之前的 π0/π0.5 用 action expert，但没人系统研究\"会不会破坏 VLM 知识\"\n- 这篇揭示问题 + 给出方案\n- 后续模型（π0.6/π*0.6/π0.7）应该都受益于这套方法\n- 是 [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/FAST-高效动作Tokenization-2026-05]] 的技术延伸——FAST 让 autoregressive 路径快，Knowledge Insulation 让 hybrid 路径稳\n\n## 关键技术细节\n\n- **作者**：Danny Driess 等 + Sergey Levine\n- **arxiv**：[2505.23705](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23705)\n- **会议**：NeurIPS 2025 poster\n- **核心**：双目标训练（FAST discrete + flow matching continuous）\n\n## 我的笔记\n\n（待补充）\n\n## See Also\n\n- [[80 知识库/具身智能/_主题页]] — 主题鸟瞰\n- [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/FAST-高效动作Tokenization-2026-05]] — Knowledge Insulation 用了 FAST tokenization 作为 discrete 路径\n- [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/π0-通用机器人策略-2026-05]] — π0 是 action expert 范式的起点\n- [[小林的/50 Learning/计划5]] — 学习清单\
…(截断)…

{
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\lnp\\Documents\\Obsidian Vault\\80 知识库\\具身智能\\_主题页.md",
  "content": "# 具身智能 · Physical Intelligence 公司技术路线\n\n> 主题页 | AI 维护 | Updated: 2026-05-23\n> 涵盖 Physical Intelligence 公司的 VLA 模型系列（π0/Hi Robot/π0.5/π*0.6/π0.7），以及支持这些主线模型的分支技术（FAST/RTC/MEM/RLT/Knowledge Insulation）和相关研究（Human Video Transfer）\n\n---\n\n## 这个主题在讨论什么\n\n**具身智能（Embodied Intelligence / Physical Intelligence）**：让 AI 系统通过物理身体与真实世界交互，完成像人类一样的日常任务。这跟下棋、写文章、画图这些\"虚拟智能\"是两个领域——物理世界有不可压缩的复杂度（**Moravec 悖论**：对 AI 来说，下棋容易、叠衣服难）。\n\n**VLA（Vision-Language-Action Model）** 是当前主流范式：从 LLM/VLM 出发，扩展出连续电机动作输出，让一个模型同时理解视觉、语言和动作。\n\n## Physical Intelligence 公司：五代主线 VLA 演进\n\n| 时间 | 模型 | 突破点 |\n|------|------|--------|\n| 2024-10 | **π0** | VLM (3B) + flow matching，首个通用机器人基础模型，控制 8 种机器人 |\n| 2025-02 | **Hi Robot** | 分层 VLA：System 2 推理 + System 1 执行，比 GPT-4o 高 40% 指令跟随 |\n| 2025-04 | **π0.5** | 单模型双输出（token + flow matching）+ 异构数据 co-training → open-world 泛化 |\n| 2025-11 | **π*0.6** | 基于 π0.6（5B backbone）+ Recap RL → 实用级 throughput（工厂连续作业） |\n| 2026-04 | **π0.7** | **emergent compositional generalization** + steerable prompts，单模型替代多 specialist |\n\n| 模型 | 关键问题 | 关键创新 | 主要任务 |\n|------|---------|---------|---------|\n| **π0** | 一个模型能控制多种机器人吗？ | VLM + flow matching | 叠衣服、收拾桌、装盒子 |\n| **Hi Robot** | 能处理复杂多步指令吗？ | 分层 VLA + 40% 高于 GPT-4o | 清桌子、做三明治、超市采购 |\n| **π0.5** | 能在没见过的家里干活吗？ | 异构数据 co-training | 全新家里清厨房卧室 |\n| **π*0.6** | 能从自己实践中改进吗？ | Recap = RL + corrections + experience | 工厂级连续作业 |\n| **π0.7** | 能组合已学技能完成新任务吗？ | Steerable prompts（language + metadata + visual subgoal）+ 多源数据蒸馏 | 用没见过的空气炸锅、跨机器人形态叠衣服 |\n\n## 分支技术（支持主线 VLA 的组件 + 重要发现）\n\n| 时间 | 技术/研究 | 类型 | 一句话 |\n|------|----------|------|--------|\n| 2025-01 | **FAST** | 工程组件 | DCT 频域压缩做动作 tokenization，π0-FAST 训练**快 5 倍** |\n| 2025-05 | **Knowledge Insulation** | 工程组件 | 保护 VLM 骨架不被 action expert \"forgetting\"，训练快 3x |\n| 2025-06 | **RTC** | 工程组件 | 异步生成 + inpaint，让大型 VLA 在 300ms+ 延迟下也能精确动作 |\n| 2025-? | **Human Video Transfer** | 科学发现 | π0.5 规模上**涌现**地学会从人类第一视角视频学技能，无需特殊机制 |\n| 2026-03 | **MEM** | 工程组件 | 短期视频 + 长期语言双模态记忆，支持 15 分钟长任务 |\n| 2026-03 | **RLT** | 工程组件 | 在 VLA 上加 RL token，几小时数据就能让精细动作加速 3x |\n\n**两条 RL 路线**：\n- **Recap（π*0.6）= 宽**：大规模 RL 提升整体性能\n- **RLT = 窄**：轻量 RL 精细补刀\n\n**两层泛化能力**：\n- **学习时**（Human Video Transfer）：scale 后能从人类视频学\n- **使用时**（π0.7 compositional generalization）：能组合已学技能完成新任务\n\n## 技术演进的关键决策\n\n### 1. 从离散 token 输出到连续动作输出（π0）\n传统 VLM 输出文字 token，但机器人需要 50Hz 的电机指令。π0 引入 flow matching（diffusion 变体）作为 action expert，让 VLM 能输出连续动作。\n\n### 2. 从单模型推理到分层推理（Hi Robot）\n复杂指令（\"清理桌子，但别动那个红色玩具\"）超出 π0 的能力。Hi Robot 加一个高层 VLM 做规划和反馈处理，让机器人有\"inner voice\"。\n\n### 3. 从分层模型回归单模型（π0.5）\nπ0.5 把分层思想内化：同一个模型既能输出 high-level 文字命令，也能输出 low-level 动作。架构更简单、效果更好。\n\n### 4. 从同环境训练到 open-world 泛化（π0.5）\n之前的 VLA 训练和测试在相似环境。π0.5 通过 co-training 异构数据，第一次实现\"在训练完全没见过的家\"里完成长任务。\n\n### 5. 从纯监督学习到 RL + 经验学习（π*0.6）\n监督学习有**复合错误**问题——小错误累积成大失败。π*0.6 用 **Recap** 三阶段训练：示范 + 纠错 + 自主经验（advantage-conditioned RL）。结果：吞吐量翻倍，失败率减半，成功率 90%+，能工厂级连
…(截断)…

## 1. Entity page："涌现能力"

**什么是 entity page**

CLAUDE.md 的"未来扩展方向"里提过：**对反复出现的概念/人物建专页**，作为跨主题的枢纽节点。跟"主题页"不同——主题页是"这片土地的总览"（具身智能、百合文学），entity page 是"这条河贯穿哪些土地"（涌现能力、Moravec 悖论、scaling laws）。

它不在任何主题目录下，可能挂在 `80 知识库/concepts/` 之类。

**为什么"涌现能力"是好候选**

- 在 [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/π0.7-Steerable通用模型-2026-05]] 里以 "compositional generalization" 形式出现
- 在 [[80 知识库/具身智能/素材/Human Video Transfer-涌现的人类视频迁移-2026-05]] 里以 "emergent capability from scale" 形式出现
- **同一个概念在两个不同侧面体现**：使用层面的涌现 vs 学习层面的涌现
- 这种"同一概念多视角"正是 entity page 的最大价值

**为什么不现在建**

- CLAUDE.md 规则：**≥5 篇素材涉及才触发**
- 现在 2 篇，**密度不够**
- 强行现在建，内容会是空泛的"涌现是 LLM 也有的特性"——没有实质交叉
- entity page 的价值来自"射线足够多形成网络"，而不是"概念被描述清楚"

**什么时候触发**

如果你之后 ingest 这些内容会立即拉高密度：
- LLM scaling laws 类文章（OpenAI/Anthropic 的论文）
- GPT-3 / GPT-4 涌现能力的研究
- AlphaGo → AlphaZero 的能力跃迁
- 你自己思考 AI 涌现现象的笔记

只要 3 篇以上落进来，"涌现能力"在 ≥5 篇素材里出现，lint 会提醒该建了。

**真建起来什么样**

```
80 知识库/concepts/涌现能力.md
├── 概念定义（什么算涌现，跟"渐进改进"的区别）
├── 在哪些素材里出现
│   ├── 具身智能领域
│   │   - π0.7 → compositional generalization
│   │   - HVT → human video learning emergence
│   ├── LLM 领域
│   │   - GPT-3 → few-shot learning emergence
│   │   - ...
├── 关键问题（涌现是真涌现还是 evaluation artifact？）
└── 跨主题联动
```

---

## 2. 主题目录规模

**规则**

CLAUDE.md lint 启发式："主题目录文章数过多（>20）→ 提议拆分"

现在 11 篇 = **55% 容量**。还有空间，但不是无限。

**为什么会提**

- 一个主题下太多文件，**人工浏览**变难（Obsidian 左侧栏滚不完）
- AI 自己读 index.md 定位时，如果"具身智能"section 有 30 条目，AI 也会**注意力分散**
- 主题页（_主题页.md）的"鸟瞰"价值随子素材数下降——现在的主题页已经有 6 个表格、17 条洞察，再加 10 篇可能臃肿

**什么时候真要拆**

| 触发 | 行动 |
|------|------|
| 文章数 > 20 | lint 提议拆 |
| 主题页 > 500 行 | 直接拆 |
| 主题里出现明显的"两类内容互不引用" | 即使没到 20 也可以拆 |

**拆的几种方式**

按"维度"拆，不是按"数量"拆：

**A. 按模型代际**
```
具身智能/
├── _主题页.md（枢纽，链接到子主题）
├── π0 系列/
│   ├── _子主题页.md
│   └── 素材/（π0, Hi Robot, π0.5, π0.6, π0.7）
└── 分支技术/
    ├── _子主题页.md
    └── 素材/（FAST, KI, RTC, MEM, RLT, HVT）
```

**B. 按"主线 vs 周边"**
不结构上拆，只在主题页里强化分类——这是现在的方式。

**C. 按公司/来源**
如果将来 ingest 别的公司（Skild AI、Figure、Physical Intelligence 之外的）：
```
具身智能/
├── Physical Intelligence/
├── Skild AI/
└── ...
```

**现在不拆的理由**

- 11 篇还没到痛点
- 主题里所有素材都来自同一公司（PI），有强一致性，拆了反而割裂语境
- 等真有"新公司加入" / "明显二类内容" 再拆

---

## 3. 跨区联动单一

**观察**

整个知识库 11 篇素材 + 主题页 + log 里 11 条 ingest 记录，**跨区 See Also 全指向同一个文件** —— `[[小林的/50 Learning/计划5]]`。

**为什么这样**

技术上：
- 我每次 ingest 都用 Grep 在 `小林的/` 里搜关键词
- 只有 `计划5` 命中（里面有"具身智能博客论文""宇树科技 论文"）
- 其他 50 Learning/ 文件名看起来不像具身智能相关

**这是好事还是坏事**

**目前是好事**：
- See Also 的价值是"精准"，不是"多"
- 强加联动（比如硬扯 [[小林的/50 Learning/学习计划]] 跟 π*0.6 的"学习类比"）会**稀释信号**
- 现状："要 See Also 就只有 计划5" → 用户知道一旦看到这个 See Also 就是真相关的

**将来会变坏的信号**：
- 你开始在 50 Learning/ 写"看完 π0 系列的思考"、"我对 VLA 的看法"等**真正的学习产出**
- 这些笔记跟知识库 PI 系列素材会有**强双向关联**——但只有你写了我才知道

**真正动起来什么样**

假设你写了 `小林的/50 Learning/对具身智能 RL 范式的思考.md`，里面提到"Recap 和 RLT 是宽窄两种 RL"。

下次我做 ingest 或 lint 时：
- Grep "Recap|RLT|具身智能" 在 `小林的/` 里命中你这篇新笔记
- 自动建议：在 `π*0.6 素材页` 和 `RLT 素材页` 末尾加 See Also → `[[小林的/50 Learning/对具身智能 RL 范式的思考]]`
- 这是**双向联动真实发生**的时刻——你的笔记 ↔ 知识库内容互相增强

**不需要现在做任何动作**——等你产生学习产出，触发自然就来。

---

## 4. 事实矛盾：✓ 无

**这一条是验证结果，但有几层含义**

**AI 是怎么查的**

启发式（heuristic），不是确定性（deterministic）。我不能保证"100% 无矛盾"，只能说"我没发现"。具体做法：

- 主线 5 篇之间，时间线连续（π0 → Hi Robot → π0.5 → π*0.6 → π0.7），后者的论述基本都明确说"基于前者"，没有打架
- 分支技术之间，各自解决独立子问题（tokenization / 延迟 / 记忆 / RL / 训练动力学），没有正面冲突
- 主线 vs 分支，分支技术大多是主线的"配套"，互相协调
- 我特别检查了 Hi Robot vs π0.5 的"分层 vs 单模型"——这曾经是矛盾点，但 π*0.6 ingest 时通过级联更新调和了

**为什么这次没有的几个原因**

1. **所有素材都来自同一公司**（PI），论述风格、概念体系统一
2. **是同一研究线的演进**，每个新模型都明确说"前作的局限是什么、我怎么解决"——这种"自指"结构防止内部矛盾
3. 还没 ingest 跨学派/跨公司的内容

**什么时候会真有矛盾**

当你 ingest 这些会立刻出现冲突：

| 来源 | 跟 PI 可能的矛盾点 |
|------|------------------|
| Google DeepMind RT-2 | "VLM + action head" 派 vs PI 的 "flow matching" 派 |
| Skild AI（一个 SOTA 不公开） | "数据规模决定一切" vs PI 的"算法 + 数据并重" |
| 波士顿动力 | "model-based control" vs PI 的 "learning-based" |
| Figure AI 等具身公司 | 数据收集策略、人形偏好等 |

**有矛盾时按 CLAUDE.md 规则处理**

> "新素材跟已有 wiki 内容矛盾时，**在主题页标注分歧和来源归属，不简单覆盖**"

举例：将来 ingest DeepMind 文章后，主题页可能出现：

```markdown
## 学派分歧

**关于 VLA 的最佳架构**：
- Physical Intelligence 派：flow matching action expert，连续输出，diffusion 思路
  - 来源：[[π0]]、[[π0.5]]、[[π*0.6]]
- Google DeepMind 派：discrete action tokens + transformer，类 LLM 思路
  - 来源：[[RT-2]]、[[Gemini Robotics]]
- 各自优势：...
```

这是知识库**真正活起来**的样子——不是"一边倒地报道一家公司"，而是**呈现领域内的张力**。

---

## 总结一句

**这 4 条都是"主动等待"型建议**——不需要你现在动作，但你之后做某些事时（写学习笔记、ingest 新公司、ingest 涌现相关内容）会自动触发动作。我下次 lint 时会主动提醒该做了。
